# M's answers to the four open questions, verbatim (2026-10-03)

To be carried into CHARTER.md once the running repair pass (which also edits the charter) has finished.

1. **Clash (d), H-INFO-S against B-RECV.** M:
   > Teleportation carries no physical substance, but does carry information (non physical properties/bounds that
   > give shape to the geometry at the seat)

   Carried as the reading **H-INFO-SHAPE**: what arrives is the defining information -- non-physical properties and
   bounds that give shape to the geometry at the seat -- and not substance. This agrees with transit.py's
   CARRIES_SUBSTANCE = False. The substance question therefore moves to the seat (the board's S13 held-seat release,
   OPEN and priced; S10 refused as a supply), and the clash with B-RECV is to be re-screened under H-INFO-SHAPE rather
   than H-INFO-S. Not seated.

2. **Weighting in the mean-value axiom.** M: "Carry both (Recommended)". Signed w (Re H) and |w| (signed Renyi) are
   both carried, each with the axioms it satisfies, until a computation or M separates them.

3. **"All its inverses and reflections".** M:
   > All of the above. Remember that the center begins at the ground state values given in real numbers from the
   > periodic table. That is the calibration

   All four are carried: the log branches, the conjugate and reciprocal, the fold to |p|, and the Radon inverse.
   The graph's origin is calibrated at ground-state values, real numbers taken from the periodic table, so a signed
   weight is a deviation from the ground state (H-ZERO's reading). Which ground-state quantities: asked of M.

4. **D67's five held OPEN rows at (0,-1,0).** M: "Keep held, noted (Recommended)". They stay as they are, each
   saying in its text that the -1 is not a bound.

## Follow-ups, verbatim (2026-10-03)

5. **H-INFO-SHAPE confirmed.** Asked whether what arrives is the defining information that shapes the geometry at
   the seat, with the physical substance supplied by the seat itself, M: "Yes, from the seat". The matter
   obstruction becomes **supply at the seat**, tested against S13 (held-seat release, OPEN, priced) and DOCKET 65
   (S10 refused as a supply). The clash with B-RECV is dissolved under this reading, not removed by assertion: the
   obstruction is relocated, and stays an obstruction until the seat's supply is shown.

6. **Calibration of the origin.** M: "Mass/ binding. But could work for any of the other options depending on the
   question being asks or the object of study". The default centre is each element's mass / binding ground (READ
   atomic masses and binding energies); the calibration is chosen per question or object of study (ground
   configurations, ionisation energies, or populate.py's axes), and every use names which calibration it takes.

## From the conversation on conversion (2026-10-04), M's words verbatim

20. **Definable condition information.** Asked what it means, M chose "Shape at the seat", and said: "All objects are
    given definition by bounds. This is a relation. Every mass states its own unique information bounds that shape it".
    On history: "Memories transfer because they are part of the descriptive conditional information about the object,
    not physical, but information nonetheless."
21. **Speed and position.** M: "speed is not a question in my work. The two positions technically exist as one. So the
    corridor allows for the the entanglement of two positions at once. The object in transit requires no speed. It is
    either at the beginning or end position, never in between them".
22. **The corridor on the board.** Asked whether the corridor should be carried as the A-B coupling (a two-position
    system |A>, |B> rotated by a coupling term) when this moves from talk to work, M: "Yes. Exactly." and "yes please".
    Recorded for when the work opens; nothing built yet.
23. **The twelve trajectories.** M: "the math for the corridor is formed by the 12 trajectories of navigation. Each one
    is a physics condition that governs an aspect of the reconstruction at seating. These 12 allow for the
    predetermination of seat compatibility based on the starting state - entanglement at 12 criteria." The 12 are the
    12-vector M named earlier (multiverse_12_vector_taxonomy_v2.pdf; settle.H12 holds each as the taxonomy defines it).
24. **What "entanglement at 12 criteria" means.** Asked whether the twelve are matched as shared values or literally
    quantum-correlated between the two positions, M chose the second ("Or you may mean the twelve conditions are
    literally quantum-correlated between the positions. - this.") and added: "The 12 conditions only need to supply
    enough for the object to reconstruct in the new environment, based on the stock of available matter, whether or not
    the environment is habitable/hospitable by the object, is not a factor." Carried as hypothesis H-12Q for the work
    on the corridor (item 22); compatibility is reconstructibility from the seat's stock, not habitability.
25. **Weak correlations and the ranking of seats.** On "Those correlations are weak and fall off sharply with distance",
    M: "this is where the magnitude of probabilities (negative and positive), and complex binary come into play. The
    stronger negative magnitudes will automatically triangulate the stronger positive magnitudes which rank the
    probability of seating". Carried as hypothesis H-SEATRANK for the corridor work (items 22-24).

## DOCKET 66 rulings (2026-10-04), M's answers verbatim

26. **Paper, CLAIMS.md 1630-1631 (Gott pair bound).** Asked whether to replace 'gamma > 1.33e5 at the bound G mu <~ 6e-7'
    with the Planck 2013 bound READ at source (G mu/c^2 < 1.5e-7, giving gamma > 5.3e5), M: "Correct it (Recommended)".

    crossing". H-TURN-CROSSING is adopted; the straight cosmic string TURNs (rays cross without focusing).
28. **Re-grading D68 on the singular-throat ruling.** Asked whether to apply M-S1A-P3 ('a singular throat is not
    disqualified'; throat creation stays OPEN) in D68's combine.py too, M: "Re-grade D68 (Recommended)". O-MAKE-TOPO
    reads OPEN via N_WNCC in D68's board-context variants as in D66's; history kept.

**Note to item 27 (2026-10-05, D66-RULINGS verifier, result.v; added after item 28, item 27 itself unchanged).** M's
words are "Any crossing", answering "any crossing of light paths". "H-TURN-CROSSING is adopted" is this file's reading
of those words, not M's: H-TURN-CROSSING's definition (docket66/defects.py HYPOTHESES) is "spec.py's PART 2 'TURN' read
as any second meeting of two geodesics from one event", which is narrower than "any crossing" by its "from one event".
Beside it, M's wording: *any crossing of light paths*. The verdict does not move: the straight string TURNs under both.
Under the definition, string_turn's parallel rays (y = +-b) are the source-at-infinity case of geodesics from one event
-- an assumption of L = b / tan(4 pi G mu), now stated; under M's wording the two rays cross, which is all it asks.

## Step 1b (2026-10-05), M's answer verbatim

29. **The balanced equation ("both sides").** Asked which two sides Step 1b's balanced equation sets against each other
    (options put: demand vs supply; theory vs measurement; both nested; corridor first), M answered in M's own words:
    "Balanced means that the value of the object at first position must equal the value of the object at the second
    position. And any variable input must be accounted for in output". Carried as the definition of Step 1b: an
    equation between the object's value at position A and its value at position B, with every variable input
    appearing in an output term. Which quantities make up "the value of the object" is not yet fixed by M's words.
30. **What "the value of the object" is.** Asked what makes up the value that must be equal at the first and second
    positions (options put: the 12-vector and the conserved quantities; the conserved quantities with Q-1 / Q-1s; the
    12 trajectories; information only), M: "Information only". So the equated value is the object's definable
    condition information; all matter and energy (what A releases, what B's stock supplies, what the transfer costs)
    sits in the input and output terms, each of which must be accounted for (item 29).
31. **Compensation in the balance.** After the first pass of Step 1b (step1b/balance.py), M: "Be sure to account for
    compensation in the physics.... Let's say mass energy density is different at the seat, but some other discrepancy
    makes up of that and dissolves the defect...". Carried as H-COMPENSATION: a discrepancy in one term between the
    first and second positions may be dissolved by an opposite discrepancy in another term, so that the balance holds
    without each term matching on its own.
32. **Is the original at A retired?** Asked whether the original at A is taken apart into stock, so the object exists
    only at B (Step 1b: on both readings sending the information does not remove the object at A), M: "I suspect so, to
    satisfy the no-cloning clause." Carried as M's hypothesis H-RETIRE-A ("I suspect": a hypothesis, not a result):
    A's instance is retired into stock at A, the output term OUT-A-RESIDUE.
33. **Which count is the object's information.** Asked which of the board's four counts (species sequence; 1 A grid;
    0.1 A grid; thermal entropy) is the object's definable condition information, M: "Keep all four". H-WHICH-COUNT
    stays open; every Step 1b figure is printed for each count.

    (1) H-EXACT-ENERGY-AT-BOUND becomes M's: the one exact energy is E(N) at the bound (C8P-O9 ruled).

    positron are produced together in beta-plus decay with their states entangled through conservation of momentum and
    spin, every energy positive.  The board's negative tension is gravitational: a plane facing ours across the warped
    bulk must read negative because the warp cannot turn back from a positive plane (SIM2-FACING T5c, S15).  Where
    negative energy meets entanglement in known physics is traversability: an entangled pair's bridge (Maldacena-Susskind,
    READ for B7) is not traversable, and making one traversable needs negative null energy from a quantum coupling
    (Gao-Jafferis-Wall 2016, standard, NOT READ) -- where 117/120 say it only appears.  Nothing is seated.

    toward 4f already at c = 137 (field 0.10029 Ha against NIST's 1.884167 eV = 0.06924 Ha).  "No constant" holds only
    for a clamped point nucleus with |q_e| = q_p: the physical atom also carries m_e/M_N, R_N/a_0 and alpha through
    spin-orbit, Breit and QED, none in M's field, and the charge ratio lambda -- which does remove La's exception (4f by
    0.730 Ha at lambda = 0.95), at the price of charged atoms.  The test also used the chain's three entrant exceptions
    (La, Ac, Th), not reading (a)'s twenty configurations.  Item 221's operational law is unaffected: it rests on M's own

    paper's eleven-element c -> infinity clause stands.  Nothing is repaired in the record of items 220-221; this entry

    yes.  Position 2's plane's radion has the kinetic coefficient C_r = (24 M^3)^2/tau2 at the coincidence
    (Pilo-Rattazzi-Zaffaroni eq. 2.16, READ via multiplane.py; g_coincide G2), negative for 139 (1)'s quarter (C_r =
    -96 at M = k_L = 1; POSITIVITY.md Q1, verified once).  Its sign is a value's, the tension's, so pointwise.py P6's
    "a ghost, a different definition, not a different value" was wrong, and it is withdrawn.  The board's readings, to
    be overruled by M at any point: (a) a kinetic coefficient takes its sign from a value where it is derived, because
    the inertia of what moves is its energy: a sheet's inertia is its tension (Nambu-Goto, computed), and a bound
    system's is its rest energy; a fundamental field's coefficient carries no value (a rescaling folds its size into
    the potential and the couplings and leaves its sign), so its sign is the definition 202 gives position 2 as ours;

    stress; (c) the record holds the separation at zero by identity of position, not by a restoring force (which would
    not cure a ghost, STATIC.md S3) -- 127 and 275 (coincident everywhere, not only while the corridor exists) and 141

    tension, and information through its carrier (101; Peres and Terno, READ: "information needs a material
    carrier"); (2) a Lorentz boost keeps every kind's invariants, the massless on the light cone at c and the massive

    1e-15) and the massless (Cassini's light, gravity's own Shapiro delay) (READ); (4) Lovelock's theorem (READ) leaves
